 {"content": "\"\"\"Tour T-05: Season Registration — Club Self-Service to Confirmation\n\nWalks the complete season registration lifecycle:\n  1. Create a season and open it\n  2. Register a team (draft registration)\n  3. Admin confirms the registration\n  4. Cancel and restore to draft\n  5. Re-confirm (idempotent close of season also exercised)\n\nKey invariants verified:\n- Season starts in 'draft', transitions to 'open' via action_open()\n- Registration state: draft → confirmed → cancelled → draft → confirmed\n- Duplicate team+season registration is rejected by DB constraint\n- Season can be closed from 'open'\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\nfrom odoo.tools.misc import mute_logger\n\n\nclass TestTourSeasonRegistration(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"T-05: Season registration lifecycle tour.\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n\n        cls.season = cls.env[\"federation.season\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Registration Tour Season\",\n                \"code\": \"REG26\",\n                \"date_start\": \"2026-01-01\",\n                \"date_end\": \"2026-12-31\",\n            }\n        )\n        cls.club = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Registration Tour Club\", \"code\": \"REGC\"}\n        )\n        cls.team = cls.env[\"federation.team\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Registration Tour Team\", \"club_id\": cls.club.id, \"code\": \"REGT\"}\n        )\n        cls.club2 = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Second Tour Club\", \"code\": \"SEC2\"}\n        )\n        cls.team2 = cls.env[\"federation.team\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Second Tour Team\", \"club_id\": cls.club2.id, \"code\": \"SETT2\"}\n        )\n\n    def test_season_registration_full_lifecycle(self):\n        \"\"\"Season registration: open season → register → confirm → cancel → re-confirm.\"\"\"\n\n        # STEP 1: Open the season\n        self.assertEqual(self.season.state, \"draft\")\n        self.season.action_open()\n        self.assertEqual(self.season.state, \"open\")\n\n        # STEP 2: Create a draft registration\n        reg = self.env[\"federation.season.registration\"].create(\n            {\n                \"season_id\": self.season.id,\n                \"team_id\": self.team.id,\n            }\n        )\n        self.assertEqual(reg.state, \"draft\")\n        self.assertTrue(reg.name)\n        self.assertNotEqual(reg.name, \"New\")\n\n        #  {"content": "# Sports Federation Discipline\n\nIncident reporting, disciplinary case management, sanctions, and player\nsuspensions. Provides the full disciplinary pipeline from incident detection\nthrough case resolution to sanction enforcement.\n\n## Purpose\n\nTracks **incidents** that occur during matches, groups them into **disciplinary\ncases** for investigation, and records **sanctions** (fines, warnings) and\n**suspensions** (match bans) as outcomes. Links back to players, clubs, and\nreferees for comprehensive disciplinary records.\n\n## Dependencies\n\n| Module | Reason |\n|--------|--------|\n| `sports_federation_base` | Clubs |\n| `sports_federation_people` | Players |\n| `sports_federation_tournament` | Matches |\n| `mail` | Chatter |\n\n## Models\n\n### `federation.match.incident`\n\nAn event that occurred during a match that may trigger disciplinary action.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Incident title |\n| `match_id` | Many2one | The match |\n| `player_id` | Many2one | Involved player |\n| `club_id` | Many2one | Involved club |\n| `referee_id` | Many2one | Reporting referee |\n| `incident_type` | Selection | warning / yellow_card / red_card / misconduct / violence / admin_issue / other |\n| `minute_text` | Char | Match minute (text for flexibility) |\n| `description` | Text | What happened |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Linked disciplinary case |\n| `date_reported` | Date | When reported |\n| `reported_by_user_id` | Many2one | System user who filed it |\n| `status` | Selection | new / attached / closed |\n\n### `federation.disciplinary.case`\n\nAn investigation triggered by one or more incidents.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Case title |\n| `reference` | Char | Auto-sequence reference number |\n| `state` | Selection | draft / under_review / decided / appealed / closed |\n| `opened_on` / `decided_on` / `closed_on` | Date | Timeline |\n| `responsible_user_id` | Many2one | Case handler |\n| `incident_ids` | One2many | Related incidents |\n| `sanction_ids` | One2many | Resulting sanctions |\n| `suspension_ids` | One2many | Resulting suspensions |\n| `incident_count` / `sanction_count` / `suspension_count` | Integer | Dedicated stat counters for case shortcuts |\n| `subject_player_id` / `subject_club_id` / `subject_referee_id` | Many2one | Subject |\n| `summary` / `notes` | Text | Case details |\n\n- **State machine**: draft → under_review → decided → appealed / closed, with under-review cases allowed back to draft for corrections before a decision is recorded.\n- **Auto-numbering** via `ir.sequence`.\n- **Counter labels** use distinct technical field names so the case relation tabs and their stat counters do not trigger duplicate-label warnings during module loading.\n\n### `federation.sanction`\n\nA penalty imposed as the outcome of a disciplinary case.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Sanction title |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Parent case |\n| `sanction_type` | Selection | fine / warning / ban / point_deduction / other |\n| `player_id` / `club_id` / `referee_id` | Many2one | Who is sanctioned |\n| `amount` / `currency_id` | Monetary | For fines |\n| `effective_date` | Date | When it takes effect |\n| `notes` | Text | Details |\n\n### `federation.suspension`\n\nA time-bound match ban for a player.\n\n| Field | Type | Description |\n|-------|------|-------------|\n| `name` | Char | Suspension title |\n| `case_id` | Many2one | Parent case |\n| `player_id` | Many2one | Suspended player |\n| `date_start` / `date_end` | Date | Ban period |\n| `state` | Selection | active / served / overturned |\n| `notes` | Text | Details |\n\n## Security Groups\n\n| Group | Purpose |\n|-------|---------|\n| Disciplinary Staff | Can manage incidents, cases, sanctions, and suspensions |\n\n## Data Files\n\n| File | Content |\n|------|---------|\n| `data/ir_sequence.xml` | `FED-DISC-` sequence for case reference numbers |\n\n## Key Behaviours\n\n1. **Incident → Case linking** — Incidents can be grouped into a case for joint handling.\n2. **Case lifecycle** — draft → under_review → decided → appealed / closed with date tracking, and under-review cases can be reopened to draft before a decision is recorded.\n3. **Multiple outcomes** — A single case can produce multiple sanctions and suspensions.\n4. **Player integration** — Inherited views add discipline tabs to player forms.\n5. **Match integration** — Inherited views add incident lists to match forms.\n6. **Finance bridge integration** — When `sports_federation_finance_bridge` is installed,\n   sanctions of type `fine` automatically create source-linked finance events and\n   keep draft amounts synchronized with sanction edits.\n", "content_length": 4687, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path":  {"content": "\"\"\"Performance regression tests for standings computation (Integration, Item 5).\n\nVerifies that _build_standing_table() is O(n + m) — the number of ORM queries\nmust not grow with the number of matches (they are fetched in one batch query).\n\nExpected query budget for _build_standing_table():\n  1. _get_relevant_matches()  → 1 query\n  2. _get_points_values()     → at most 1 query (rule_set already cached)\n  3. _get_participants()      → 1 query\nTotal: <= 5 queries regardless of match count.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom odoo.tests.common import TransactionCase\n\n\nclass TestStandingsPerformance(TransactionCase):\n    \"\"\"O(1) participant-lookup regression test for _build_standing_table().\"\"\"\n\n    @classmethod\n    def setUpClass(cls):\n        super().setUpClass()\n        cls.has_result_control = (\n            \"include_in_official_standings\" in cls.env[\"federation.match\"]._fields\n        )\n        cls.club = cls.env[\"federation.club\"].create(\n            {\"name\": \"Perf Club\", \"code\": \"PCL\"}\n        )\n        cls.season = cls.env[\"federation.season\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Perf Season\",\n                \"code\": \"PSN\",\n                \"date_start\": \"2025-01-01\",\n                \"date_end\": \"2025-12-31\",\n            }\n        )\n        cls.rule_set = cls.env[\"federation.rule.set\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Perf Rule Set\",\n                \"code\": \"PRS\",\n                \"points_win\": 3,\n                \"points_draw\": 1,\n                \"points_loss\": 0,\n            }\n        )\n        cls.tournament = cls.env[\"federation.tournament\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Perf Tournament\",\n                \"code\": \"PTOUR\",\n                \"season_id\": cls.season.id,\n                \"date_start\": \"2025-06-01\",\n                \"rule_set_id\": cls.rule_set.id,\n            }\n        )\n\n        # Create 10 teams and register them as participants\n        cls.teams = cls.env[\"federation.team\"].create(\n            [\n                {\"name\": f\"Perf Team {i}\", \"club_id\": cls.club.id, \"code\": f\"PT{i}\"}\n                for i in range(10)\n            ]\n        )\n        cls.participants = cls.env[\"federation.tournament.participant\"].create(\n            [{\"tournament_id\": cls.tournament.id, \"team_id\": t.id} for t in cls.teams]\n        )\n\n        # Create 200 done matches between team pairs (cycling through all pairs)\n        teams = cls.teams\n        n = len(teams)\n        match_vals = []\n        count = 0\n        # Generate round-robin pairs and repeat until 200 matches\n        pairs = [(teams[i], teams[j]) for i in range(n) for j in range(n) if i != j]\n        while count < 200:\n            home, away = pairs[count % len(pairs)]\n            vals = {\n                \"tournament_id\": cls.tournament.id,\n                \"home_team_id\": home.id,\n                \"away_team_id\": away.id,\n                \"home_score\": (count % 4),\n                \"away_score\": (count % 3),\n                \"state\": \"done\",\n            }\n            if cls.has_result_control:\n                vals[\"include_in_official_standings\"] = True\n            match_vals.append(vals)\n            count += 1\n        cls.env[\"federation.match\"].create(match_vals)\n\n        cls.standing = cls.env[\"federation.standing\"].create(\n            {\n                \"name\": \"Perf Standing\",\n                \"tournament_id\": cls.tournament.id,\n                \"rule_set_id\": cls.rule_set.id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def test_build_standing_table_query_count(self):\n        \"\"\"_build_standing_table() must not issue more than 10 queries for 200 matches.\n\n        The fixed O(n+m) implementation fetches all participants once and all\n        matches once; subsequent lookups are dict-based and require no further\n        DB round-trips.\n        \"\"\"\n        with self.assertQueryCount(10):\n            stats = self.standing._build_standing_table()\n\n        # Sanity: all 10 participants have entries\n        self.assertEqual(len(stats), 10)\n\n    def test_build_standing_table_correct_totals(self):\n        \"\"\"Verify stats are correct for 200 matches (not just a query-count concern).\"\"\"\n        stats = self.standing._build_standing_table()\n        total_played = sum(s[\"played\"] for s in stats.values())\n        # Each match contributes 1 to home's played and 1 to away's played\n        self.assertEqual(total_played, 200 * 2)\n", "content_length": 4339, "filesystem_mode": "0644", "git_mode": "100644", "git_state": "tracked", "path": "sports_federation_standings/tests/test_standings_performance.py", "record_type": "file", "sha256": "2d67196e5a84b2a1bc90dac415a72e310510815ac5bb053b98649c60791dc358", "size_bytes": 4347}
